# V17 complete original discovery and boundary register

These accepted V17 supporting registers serve the existing Humor/Voice ledger responsibilities as volume evidence appendices. Publication and whole-tranche adjudication remain separate. Original discovery, assignments and authored-time statements remain exact; current errata are explicitly external. Same-model conditional independence and receiving limits are recorded in the [V17 receiving review](../09%20Audits%20and%20Manifests/KONOSUBA_V17_RECEIVING_REVIEW.md). Exact source members are text/part0009.html (C1), part0010.html (C2), part0011.html (C3), part0012.html (F), part0013.html (Ep); excluded bodies/images/holdouts remain unopened in this continuation.

This is the single complete public home for all 342 original discovery objects,179 fixed whole-premise boundaries,28 explicit operations, all original routes/flags and43 scope-touched units. Counts are provenance and denominator metadata, not independent trials. [Complete paired coding](KONOSUBA_V17_HUMOR_CODING_REGISTER.md) and [voice navigation](KONOSUBA_V17_VOICE_COMPARISON_REGISTER.md) retain later formal histories.

Each table value is compact JSON with one reversible HTML escape for &,<,>,|; JSON carries literal newline/backslash/quote escapes. {"@exact_text":"ID"} expands to the exact string in the named catalog. This is lossless factoring of repeated text, not a shortened rationale. Every unique initial rationale is printed in full, in its own labeled row; its value is factored out of the otherwise exact initial field bundle and restored by the inverse proof. Proof parses actual Markdown rows, reverses escapes/references, and compares every projected field to the immutable initial inputs. Private full novel bases/windows, ruby XML/pairs and long selected quotations are omitted by the declared projection; their hashes/locators and initial analytical metadata remain. No global spacing/reflow is applied to original strings. Native literal joins verify text and location, not by themselves speaker, addressee, literal-gloss accuracy or pragmatic judgment; those remain substantive receiving responsibilities.

This file uses its own HD catalog below. “NOT_ASSIGNED”/null fields remain exact historical uncoded metadata, not live uncompleted coding. Root fixed-input acceptance externally governs the unchanged proposal-only fields.

Complete exact original metadata and routes are partitioned by their original discovery lineage: [Producer 185 originals](KONOSUBA_V17_HUMOR_DISCOVERY_PRODUCER_ORIGINALS.md) and [Independent 157 originals](KONOSUBA_V17_HUMOR_DISCOVERY_INDEPENDENT_ORIGINALS.md). The partitions use this home’s HD catalog and retain every object; they are not result-selected.

| HD0002 | "A costly Lightning exchange leaves Kazuma badly wounded and his magic sword shattered saving him; called lucky by the King, he complains true luck would avoid this and blames Dust’s dud gift, making even the King object." |
| HD0003 | "A knight recognizes Kazuma’s notorious name; Aqua lists defeated officers and his sneaky methods until the room fears he may already be there, while the actual hidden-in-plain-sight Kazuma resents both the insults and tactically dangerous warning." |
| HD0004 | "A prebattle recruitment memory prompts harem speculation and the stolen royal ring panic; Kazuma admits storing it with porn while Darkness redirects the final kill for an unexplained reason, misread as reward greed." |
| HD0005 | "A shallow poisoned surprise stab drops Mamon while Kazuma calls the assassination skill tailor-made; the guard protests this supposed human’s cruelty and quickly regroups." |
